//! Release-only storage outside the active row/index namespace.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#2-retain-by-default — moves, restorations and deletion evidence share activation's transaction.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType, materialize_optional_fields};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

pub(crate) const VERSION: &str = "snapback4-release-store-3";

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct Column {
    kind: ColumnType,
    default: Option<ColumnDefault>,
    authoritative: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    capture_source: Option<Table>,
}

pub(crate) fn enable(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_narrow_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_tables(table_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, definition TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_columns(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_values(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_indexes(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,index_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,index_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_refs(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,invalid INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_parent ON _dormant_refs(parent_t,parent_id);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_events(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,at INTEGER NOT NULL,operation TEXT NOT NULL,release INTEGER,previous INTEGER,generation INTEGER NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_event_items(event INTEGER NOT NULL,path TEXT NOT NULL,detail TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(event,path)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    if !live_cells_column(conn)? {
        // Published predecessors have captured-only cells. Defaulting them
        // to captured is conservative; shared definitions cannot prove a
        // particular retained cell ever had live authority.
        conn.execute_batch("ALTER TABLE _dormant_values ADD COLUMN live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;")?;
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version' AND value!='snapback4-release-store-5'", [VERSION])?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn live_cells_column(conn:&Connection)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pragma_table_info('_dormant_values') WHERE name='live_source')",[],|r|r.get(0))?)
}

pub(super) fn enabled(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(matches!(tx.meta("version")?.as_deref(),Some(VERSION|"snapback4-release-store-2"|super::backfills::VERSION)))
}

fn authoritative(table: &Table, column: &str) -> bool {
    !table.maintained.contains_key(column) && !table.engine_field(column)
}

pub(super) fn definition(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, table: &Table, column: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let value = Column {
        kind: table.columns[column].clone(),
        default: table.defaults.get(column).cloned(),
        authoritative: authoritative(table, column),
        capture_source: table.engine_field(column).then(||table.clone()),
    };
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_columns VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,column_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition", params![name,column,canonical_json(&value)])?;
    Ok(())
}

fn references(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => references(inner, value, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) => {
            for value in values {
                references(of, value, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_reference(
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
            *id = next.clone();
            true
        }
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_reference(inner, value, ids),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_row(
    table: &Table,
    row: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    let (Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields)) = row else { return false };
    table.columns.iter().fold(false, |changed, (column, kind)| {
        fields.get_mut(column).is_some_and(|value| rewrite_legacy_reference(kind, value, ids)) || changed
    })
}

/// Rewrite references retained outside the active namespace alongside their
/// active counterparts. References whose aggregate target was itself retired
/// have no map and retain the ordinary dormant-lifetime refusal on restore.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#3.0 — identity adoption covers every surviving typed reference, including dormant storage.
pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    if ids.values().all(BTreeMap::is_empty) || !enabled(tx)? { return Ok(0); }
    let mut rewritten = 0_u64;

    let cells = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT v.t,v.id,v.column_name,v.data,c.definition FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog k ON k.num=v.t JOIN _dormant_columns c ON c.table_name=k.name AND c.column_name=v.column_name ORDER BY v.t,v.id,v.column_name"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?, row.get::<_,String>(4)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, column, data, definition) in cells {
        let definition: Column = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_reference(&definition.kind, &mut value, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4", params![canonical_json(&value),t,id,column])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let rows = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT r.t,r.id,r.data,d.definition FROM _dormant_rows r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t JOIN _dormant_tables d ON d.table_name=c.name ORDER BY r.t,r.id"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, data, definition) in rows {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
        tx.conn().prepare(
            "SELECT t,id,data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows ORDER BY t,id"
        )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?
    } else { Vec::new() };
    for (t, id, data, definition) in narrowed {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    for (target, target_ids) in ids {
        if target_ids.is_empty() { continue; }
        let parent_t = tx.num(target)?;
        for (old, next) in target_ids {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id,invalid) SELECT t,id,column_name,parent_t,?1,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3", params![next,parent_t,old])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2", params![parent_t,old])?;
        }
    }
    Ok(rewritten)
}

pub(super) fn capture_refs(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
        params![t, id, column],
    )?;
    capture_refs_preserving(tx,t,id,column,kind,value,&BTreeSet::new())
}

pub(super) fn capture_refs_preserving(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,kind:&ColumnType,value:&Value,known:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    let mut refs=Vec::new();references(kind,value,&mut refs);
    for(parent,parent_id)in refs {if !known.contains(&parent_id){add_reference(tx,t,id,column,&parent,&parent_id)?;}}
    Ok(())
}

fn add_reference(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    parent: &str,
    parent_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
        && !tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
            params![parent_t, parent_id],
            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
        )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    table: &Table,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    row: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
            || matches!(
                column,
                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
            ))
            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
        {
            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn save_cell(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    table: &Table,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    value: &Value,
    capture: bool,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let t = tx.num(name)?;
    // @ref LLP 3000.014.000#implementation-proof-clarifications — another row cannot overwrite a cell's live origin.
    let live=table.engine_field(column) && table.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some());
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_values(t,id,column_name,data,live_source) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5) ON CONFLICT(t,id,column_name) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data,live_source=excluded.live_source",params![t,id,column,canonical_json(value),i64::from(live)])?;
    if capture && !super::authority_origin::live_input(table,column) {
        capture_refs(tx, t, id, column, &table.columns[column], value)
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn refuse(message: String) -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_ADOPT",
        message,
    }
}

pub(super) fn check_owner(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, old: &Table, next: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let Some(new) = next.table(name) else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    let evidence = || -> Result<String, RuntimeError> {
        let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
        super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, _| {
            samples.add(id, "storage ownership");
            Ok(())
        })?;
        if samples.count == 0 {
            let t = tx.num(name)?;

exec
/bin/bash -lc "timeout 10s sed -n '300,610p' snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/dormant.rs" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-mig
 succeeded in 0ms:
            evidence()?
        )));
    }
    for column in old
        .columns
        .keys()
        .filter(|column| authoritative(old, column))
    {
        if new.columns.contains_key(column) && !authoritative(new, column) {
            return Err(refuse(format!(
                "column {name}.{column}: {}; authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured values; rename the new column, or retire it, run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive`, then deploy",
                evidence()?
            )));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Restore all parents before checking any retained child's lifetime. Consume
/// only candidate-visible cells, so partial table restoration is reversible.
pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    restore: bool,
    now:i64,
    report: &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
) -> Result<BTreeSet<String>, RuntimeError> {
    if !enabled(tx)? {
        return Ok(BTreeSet::new());
    }
    if !super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? || !live_cells_column(tx.conn())? {enable(tx.conn())?;}
    super::backfills::prepare_inputs(tx,next,previous)?;
    let whole: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
        .conn()
        .prepare("SELECT table_name,definition FROM _dormant_tables ORDER BY table_name")?
        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut restoring = Vec::new();
    let mut retained_through = BTreeSet::new();
    for (name, text) in &whole {
        let old: Table =
            serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        check_owner(tx, name, &old, next)?;
        if next.tables.contains_key(name) {
            if old.through.is_some() {
                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
            }
            restoring.push((name.clone(), old));
        }
    }
    let cells:Vec<(String,String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT table_name,column_name,definition FROM _dormant_columns ORDER BY table_name,column_name")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
    let mut reuse: Vec<String> = restoring.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();
    for (name, column, text) in &cells {
        if let Some(table) = next.table(name).filter(|t| t.columns.contains_key(column)) {
            if column == crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE {
                retained_through.insert(name.clone());
            }
            let old: Column =
                serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            if let Some(source)=&old.capture_source {
                if source.through.is_some() && source.through == table.through {retained_through.insert(name.clone());}
                else if source.through.as_ref().zip(table.through.as_ref()).is_some_and(|(a,b)| (a.live.is_some() || b.live.is_some()) && a.reference==b.reference && a.parent==b.parent) { /* Restamp destination; live provenance never retains historical authority. */ }
                else if source.through.is_some() && table.through.is_some() {return Err(refuse(format!("{name}: retained engine captures belong to another read-through definition; restore the original definition before changing the audience")));}
            }
            if old.authoritative && !authoritative(table, column) {
                let mut samples = super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
                let t = tx.num(name)?;
                let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare(
                    "SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 ORDER BY id",
                )?;
                for row in statement.query_map(params![t, column], |r| {
                    Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
                })? {
                    let (id, value) = row?;
                    samples.add(&id, &super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&value));
                }
                return Err(refuse(format!(
                    "column {name}.{column}: {}; dormant authoritative values cannot become maintained/captured; rename the new column, or run `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` while it is dormant, then deploy",
                    samples.describe()
                )));
            }
            if previous.and_then(|s|s.table(name)).is_none_or(|t|!t.columns.contains_key(column)) {reuse.push(format!("{name}.{column}"));}
        }
    }
    let indexes: Vec<(String, String)> = tx
        .conn()
        .prepare(
            "SELECT table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes ORDER BY table_name,index_name",
        )?
        .query_map([], |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    for (name, index) in &indexes {
        if next
            .table(name)
            .is_some_and(|t| t.indexes.contains_key(index))
        {
            reuse.push(format!("{name}.{index}"));
        }
    }
    if !restore && !reuse.is_empty() {
        let inventory = super::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        return Err(refuse(format!(
            "dormant names [{}] require restoration intent; retained storage: {}; proceed with `snapback4 deploy <dir> --restore-dormant`, or `snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive` before fresh creation",
            reuse.join(", "),
            inventory.iter().map(|i|format!("{}: {} rows; sample ids [{}]",i.key,i.rows,i.sample_ids.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")
        )));
    }
    let backfilling=!next.backfills.is_empty() || super::backfills::has_evidence(tx)?;
    if let Some(previous) = previous {
        for (name, old) in previous
            .tables
            .iter()
            .filter(|(n, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none() && previous.maintain_for(n).is_none())
        {
            check_owner(tx, name, old, next)?;
            let candidate = next.table(name);
            let removed: Vec<_> = old
                .columns
                .keys()
                .filter(|c| candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.columns.contains_key(*c)))
                .cloned()
                .collect();
            for column in &removed {
                definition(tx, name, old, column)?;
            }
            for (index, definition) in &old.indexes {
                if candidate.is_none_or(|t| !t.indexes.contains_key(index)) {
                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_indexes VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,index_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition",params![name,index,canonical_json(definition)])?;
                }
            }
            if candidate.is_none() {
                tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_tables VALUES(?1,?2) ON CONFLICT(table_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition",params![name,canonical_json(old)])?;
            }
            if candidate.is_none() && backfilling {continue;} // Backfills require the complete source closure.
            if removed.is_empty() && candidate.is_some() {
                continue;
            }
            super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |id, mut row| {
                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, old);
                if candidate.is_none() {
                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(t,id) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",params![tx.num(name)?,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
                    for (column, kind) in &old.columns {
                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                            if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {capture_refs(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, kind, value)?;}
                            capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    for column in &removed {
                        if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                            save_cell(tx, name, old, id, column, value, true)?;
                            capture_through_parent(tx, name, old, id, column, &row)?;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Ok(())
            })?;
            if candidate.is_none() {
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1",[tx.num(name)?])?;
                for index in old.indexes.keys(){tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?])?;}
            }
        }
    }
    for (name, old) in &restoring {
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        let table = &next.tables[name];
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) SELECT t,id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",
            [t],
        )?;
        for column in old
            .columns
            .keys()
            .filter(|c| !table.columns.contains_key(*c))
        {
            definition(tx, name, old, column)?;
        }
        super::activation::walk(tx, t, |id, row| {
            let removed: Vec<_> = row
                .as_object()?
                .iter()
                .filter(|(c, _)| !table.columns.contains_key(*c))
                .map(|(c, v)| (c.clone(), v.clone()))
                .collect();
            for (column, value) in removed {
                if old.columns.contains_key(&column) {
                    save_cell(tx, name, old, id, &column, &value, false)?;
                }
            }

            // Full source image/deadlines stay intact until lifetime retirement and narrowed classification.
            Ok(())
        })?;
        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'table',t,'',id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
            if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',t,'',id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;}
        } else {
            let ids=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
            tx.set_meta(&format!("activation_table_restored_ids:{name}"),&serde_json::to_string(&ids).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
        }
        tx.conn()
            .execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1", [t])?;
        tx.conn()
            .execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_tables WHERE table_name=?1", [name])?;
    }
    let mut authority_sources=previous.map(|s|s.tables.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
    for (name,table) in &restoring {authority_sources.insert(name.clone(),table.clone());}
    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    for (name, table) in next.tables.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        if !cells
            .iter()
            .any(|(n, c, _)| n == name && table.columns.contains_key(c))
            && !restoring.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == name)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let old=authority_sources.get(name);
        let parent_scratch=if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
            old.map_or(0,|old|crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,&authority_sources,name,old))
        } else {0};
        let scratch=|row:&Value|->Result<crate::value::InternalPrice,RuntimeError> {
            let fetch=super::authority_origin::forward_fetch(tx,t,row.field("id")?.as_str()?,old,table)?;
            Ok(crate::value::InternalPrice::zero().fixed(if fetch {parent_scratch} else {0}))
        };
        super::activation_walk::walk_metered_checked(tx,t,crate::rows::adoption::Work::DECODE,if parent_scratch>0 {Some(&scratch)} else {None},|id,mut row,meter| {
            let old=authority_sources.get(name);
            super::backfills::record_omissions(tx,t,id,&row)?;
            if let Some(old)=old {
                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,old);
                super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,&authority_sources,name,old,&row,now,false)?;
                if restoring.iter().any(|(n,_)|n==name) {super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,id,old)?;}
            }
            let values=restored_values(tx,t,id,table,meter)?;
            super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,id,old,table,&mut row,values)?;
            tx.conn().execute(
                "UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",
                params![canonical_json(&row), t, id],
            )?;
            Ok(())
        })?;
    }
    for name in next.tables.keys() {
        if next.tables[name].through.is_some() && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1)",[tx.num(name)?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {retained_through.insert(name.clone());}
    }
    let mut source_definitions=previous.map(|s|s.tables.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
    for (name,table) in &restoring {source_definitions.insert(name.clone(),table.clone());}
    crate::engine::through_activation::forward_sources(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    for (name,column,text) in &cells {
        if let Some(candidate)=next.table(name).filter(|t|t.columns.contains_key(column) || next.backfills.iter().any(|f|f.table==*name && f.inputs.contains_key(column))) {
            let old:Column=serde_json::from_str(text).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            let source=source_definitions.entry(name.clone()).or_insert_with(||candidate.clone());
            if let Some(capture)=&old.capture_source {
                source.through=capture.through.clone();source.expire=capture.expire.clone();

            }
            if super::live_activation::active(tx)? && authority_sources.get(name).is_none_or(|t|t.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some())) {
                // Cell provenance survives shared definition changes. Immediate
                // manual correspondence above still precedes this marker.
                tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored SELECT 'live-authority',v.t,'',v.id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND v.column_name=?2 AND v.live_source=1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=v.t AND n.id=v.id)",params![tx.num(name)?,column])?;
            }
            source.columns.insert(column.clone(),old.kind);
            if let Some(default)=old.default {source.defaults.insert(column.clone(),default);}
            if !old.authoritative {
                if let Some(owner)=candidate.maintained.get(column) {source.maintained.insert(column.clone(),owner.clone());}
                if let Some(capture)=candidate.recorded.get(column) {source.recorded.insert(column.clone(),capture.clone());}
            }
        }
    }
    let restored_images=!restoring.is_empty() || cells.iter().any(|(n,c,_)|next.table(n).is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)));
    // Current authority cells precede destination narrowing: historical
    // authored copies may be outside a newly narrowed type even though the
    // current parent value fits it. Newly restored narrowed rows reconcile
    // individually inside transition as well.
    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    if backfilling {
        super::narrowed::restore_images(tx,next,now,&source_definitions,&authority_sources,true)?;
        super::backfills::retire_snapshot(tx,&source_definitions,previous,now)?;
    }else{super::narrowed::retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;}
    // Archive removed tables only after source expiry has closed over every
    // predecessor child. Capture the surviving post-cascade/set-null image.
    if backfilling {if let Some(previous)=previous {for (name,old) in &previous.tables {
        if next.tables.contains_key(name) || old.ephemeral.is_some() || previous.maintain_for(name).is_some(){continue;}
        let t=tx.num(name)?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,old);
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_rows VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,canonical_json(&row)])?;
            for (column,kind) in &old.columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
                if !super::authority_origin::live_input(old,column) {capture_refs(tx,t,id,column,kind,value)?;}
                capture_through_parent(tx,name,old,id,column,&row)?;
            }}Ok(())
        })?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM r WHERE t=?1",[t])?;
        for index in old.indexes.keys(){tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?])?;}
    }}}
    super::backfills::transition(tx,next,previous,&source_definitions,now,report)?;
    super::narrowed::transition(tx,next,previous,now,restored_images,&source_definitions,&authority_sources)?;
    crate::engine::through_activation::reconcile_manual(tx,next,&authority_sources,now)?;
    check_references(tx, next,&authority_sources)?;
    // Restored rows have no predecessor indexes. Install the identity scan
    // before the ordinary read-through barrier; preserve historical captures.
    let identity_tables:BTreeSet<String>=if tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true") {
        next.tables.iter().filter(|(n,t)|t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(n).is_none()).map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()
    } else {restoring.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.clone()).collect()};
    for name in &identity_tables {
        let table = &next.tables[name];
        let t = tx.num(name)?;
        let index = tx.num(&format!("{name}.byId"))?;
        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[index])?;

codex
The code separates conflict checks on current row images from evaluation on reconstructed historical images, with patches staged before application. I’m tracing the ordering around restoration and expiry, then checking how live activation and preview obtain their clock authority.

//! A process-local clock for development and explicitly classified staging stores.
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const MAX_TIME: i64 = snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX;

pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }
pub fn release() -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY", "clock", "clock controls require dev or a deploy --staging store; production always uses wall time").guide("clock") }

/// Isolated time tests stay inside an assigned range, or request a free port.
pub(crate) fn isolated_command() -> String {
    isolated_range(std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok().as_deref(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok().as_deref())
}
fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    }
}

pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
}

pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
}

fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
}

pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    match text {
        None => Ok(now),
        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Clock {
    dev: bool,
    base: i64,
    offset: i64,
    started: Instant,
    epoch: String,
    revision: u64,
    #[cfg(test)]
    frozen: bool,
}

impl Clock {
    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
        Ok(Self { dev, offset, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    }
    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    pub fn offset(&self) -> i64 { self.offset }
    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    }
    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = 0;
        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        self.started = Instant::now();
        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
        Ok(self.base)
    }
    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
        let offset = self.offset.checked_add(milliseconds).ok_or_else(|| input("cumulative clock offset overflow"))?;
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = offset;
        self.base = next;
        self.started = Instant::now();
        Ok(next)
    }
}

/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
        }
        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let release_path = options.flags.get("release").map(|dir| std::path::PathBuf::from(dir).join("store.sqlite"));
        if release_path.is_some() && options.flags.contains_key("port") { return Err("clock --release does not accept --port".into()); }
        let (address, instance, token) = if let Some(path) = &release_path {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
            let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(path).ok_or("staging clock requires a running serve")?;
            let session = crate::serve::token::mint_live(path, "clock", 1).map_err(|(_,error)|error)?;
            (address,None,Some(session.token))
        } else { let (address,instance)=crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?; (address,instance,None) };
        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
        let response = if let Some(token) = &token {
            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_authorized(address,"POST","/dev/clock/advance",None,Some(token),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
        } else { crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)) };
        let (status, json) = response.map_err(|error| {
            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
        })?;
        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
        }
        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
        Ok(json.to_string())
    })();
    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod isolated_tests {
    #[test]
    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {
        assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(Some("24000"), Some("24009")), "dev --memory --port 24000-24009 within the current range");
        for (lo,hi) in [(None,None),(Some("0"),Some("3")),(Some("4"),Some("3")),(Some("bad"),Some("3"))] {
            assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(lo,hi), "dev --memory --port 0");
        }
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    lock(&shared.engine).store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
}
fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
        Ok(service) => service,
        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
    };
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        if let Some(principal) = &service {
            if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
  /** Authoritative staging time; requires a staging store and local operator authority. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
  /** Advance staging time once and drain due work. A lost reply must not be retried. */
  advance(duration: string, options?: { eachBoundary?: boolean }): Promise<number>;
  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;
  seedAccounts(options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions>;
  sessions?: StagingSessions;
}
export interface ServeOptions extends ProofOptions { seedUsers?: SeedAccountsOptions; clockOffset?: string }
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && (typeof opts.clockOffset !== "string" || !/^[+-]?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(opts.clockOffset))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: clockOffset is a signed duration such as +25h");
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && opts.args?.some(arg => arg === "--clock-offset" || arg.startsWith("--clock-offset="))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: pass clockOffset once");
  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], { ...opts, args: [...(opts.args ?? []), ...(opts.clockOffset === undefined ? [] : ["--clock-offset", opts.clockOffset])] });
  const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<number> => {
    const config = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/auth/config`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!config.response.ok || config.body.environment !== "staging") throw Error("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY: clock controls require a deploy --staging store");
    const clockToken = served.operatorToken("clock");
    const response = await fetch(`${host.url}/dev/clock/${body ? "advance" : "status"}`, { method: body ? "POST" : "GET", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await clockToken}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
    const result = await response.json() as { now?: number; denied?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
    if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Error(`staging clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect clock and queued work before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`);
    return result.now!;
  };
  const served: ServeHost = { ...host,
    now: () => clockCall(),
    advance: (duration, options = {}) => {
      if (typeof duration !== "string" || !/^\+?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(duration)) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: advance requires a positive whole duration");
      return clockCall({ duration, eachBoundary: options.eachBoundary ?? false });
    },
    operatorToken: async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--release", directory, "--quiet"], opts);
      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
      const token = result.stdout.trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof operator token malformed");
      return token;
    },
    seedAccounts: accountOptions => seedAccounts(served, accountOptions),
  };
  try { if (opts.seedUsers) served.sessions = await served.seedAccounts(opts.seedUsers); return served; }
  catch (error) { await served.stop(); throw error; }
}
async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".");
  const directory = resolve(cwd, dir);
  // Vite is resolved from the app, never the proof package's development dependencies.
  const vite = kind === "vite" ? createRequire(resolve(directory, "package.json")).resolve("vite") : "";
  const run = logged(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-host.js", import.meta.url)), kind, directory, ports, vite], { ...options, env, detached: process.platform !== "win32", livenessPipe: true });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_PROOF_READY (\{.*\})$/);
      if (!match) return;
      const data = JSON.parse(match[1]!);
      if (data.pid !== run.child.pid || data.kind !== kind) throw Error("proof frontend PID mismatch");
      return data.url as string;
    }, timeout);
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
    await response.body?.cancel();
    return { url, pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
/** @throws missing export/index or build script precondition; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startStatic(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("static", dir, options); }
/** @throws missing Vite dependency/configuration; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startVite(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("vite", dir, options); }

export interface CliResult { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: ProofHost["logs"] }
/** Waits for close (stdio drained), never merges the two streams. Nonzero is returned for refusal assertions. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export function cli(args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  return command(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("../bin/snapback4.js", import.meta.url)), ...args], { ...options, secret: options.secret || args[0] === "token" });
}

  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}
#[test]
fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("B20".into()));
}
#[test]
fn cross_reference_existence_is_evaluated_before_any_table_output_changes(){
 let old="table sightings:\n  tower: text <=20\n  reportedAt: time\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  tower:","  confidence: enum('confirmed','possible')\n  tower:")+"backfill sightings confidence(tower: text <=20, reportedAt: time):\n  {confidence:'confirmed' if exists sightings where .tower != tower and .reportedAt >= reportedAt - 15m and .reportedAt <= reportedAt + 15m else 'possible'}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"a","tower":"north","reportedAt":1000000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"b","tower":"south","reportedAt":1100000}));seed(&mut e,"sightings",json!({"id":"c","tower":"west","reportedAt":9000000}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","a","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","b","confidence"),Value::String("confirmed".into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"sightings","c","confidence"),Value::String("possible".into()));
}
#[test]
fn retained_table_reentry_never_feeds_a_transform_its_own_output(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for direct_old in [false,true] {
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",3).unwrap();
  if direct_old {adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
  else {adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(3));}
 }
}
#[test]
fn previously_narrowed_rows_join_the_backfill_source_snapshot(){
 let old="table facts:\n  principal: principal\n  n: int 0..20\n  unique principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("int 0..20","int 0..5");
 let next=old.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int").replace("  owner: principal","  score: int\n  owner: principal")+"backfill facts twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\nbackfill bookings scores(owner: principal):\n  {score:(facts[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"facts",json!({"id":"a","principal":"user:a","n":9}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("facts").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&next,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"facts","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","b","score"),Value::Int(9));
}
#[test]
fn historical_lookup_duplicates_refuse_before_another_patch_can_resolve_them(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  replacement: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  principal: principal","  unique principal\n  principal: principal").replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill members uniquePrincipals(replacement: principal):\n  {principal:replacement}\nbackfill bookings plots(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);for(id,replacement,plot)in[("a","user:a","A"),("b","user:b","B")]{seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":id,"principal":"user:x","replacement":replacement,"plot":plot}));}
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"c","owner":"user:x"}));let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("historical lookup")&&error.contains("not unique")&&error.contains("2 affected rows"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn pruning_preimages_is_visible_and_exact_reversal_then_refuses(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";let next=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 let preview=e.store_mut().prune_dormant(false,None).unwrap();assert!(preview["items"].as_array().unwrap().iter().any(|i|i["kind"]=="backfill"&&i["rows"]==1));
 e.store_mut().prune_dormant(true,None).unwrap();let error=adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("explicitly pruned"),"{error}");assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));
}
#[test]
fn carried_backfills_apply_to_rows_restored_for_the_first_time(){
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  label: enum('a','b')\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let narrow=old.replace("enum('a','b')","enum('a')");
 let with_fill=narrow.replace("  n: int","  doubled: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {doubled:n * 2}\n";
 let restored=with_fill.replace("enum('a')","enum('a','b')");
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":9,"label":"b"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&narrow,2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&with_fill,3).unwrap();assert_eq!(e.store().count("items").unwrap(),0);
 adopt(&mut e,&restored,4).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","doubled"),Value::Int(18));
}
