    /// Remove a row the engine itself retires (an expired one): no rule and
    /// no constraint stands between the engine and its own sweep, while
    /// aggregates and partition events apply as for any delete.
    pub(super) fn remove(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table_name, Effect::Delete)?;
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing table {table_name}")))?;
        let entries = self.maintain_scratch(self.contribution_heap(table), |runner| {
            runner.tx.delete(table_name, table, old)
        })?;
        self.charge_source_indexes(table, Some(old), None, entries)?;
        self.row_changed(table_name, Some(old), None, Origin::Source)
    }

    /// Physically retire one row whose upper lifetime ended. Expiry bypasses
    /// the root's authored delete rule and constraints, but it does not bypass
    /// the schema's declared effects on rows that reference the root. A
    /// read-through child still retains the expired parent physically.
    // @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — expiry cleanup runs the same closure/restrict/set-null actions as an authored delete.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_row(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if self.physical_children(table_name, old.field("id")?)? { return Ok(false); }
        let current = if self.schema.is_referenced(table_name) {
            self.apply_reference_actions(table_name, old)?;
            // A cascade may maintain a column on the root; delete its current
            // physical image so index and cap entries match what is stored.
            let id = old.field("id")?.as_str()?;
            self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))?
                .unwrap_or_else(|| old.clone())
        } else {
            old.clone()
        };
        self.remove(table_name, &current)?;
        Ok(true)
    }

    /// At a program commit's end, retire every source row the commit changed
    /// or inserted whose lifetime has already ended: its final
    /// image was never live at a commit boundary, so it leaves no row for a
    /// later sweep to announce. Inside the commit the
    /// row existed as written (an insert-then-update still works). Engine
    /// bookkeeping, unmetered: the program's metering is already recorded.
    /// Server execution and device prediction both run it so a predicted
    /// final image cannot omit cascade, set-null or restrict effects. Its work,
    /// returned, stays
    /// linear in the rows retired: each aggregate row takes one net change
    /// and each audience key is scanned for a live row once. Seeds and loads
    /// ordinarily keep their rows for the sweep; row-keyed view sources use
    /// `retire_seeded_view_sources` to preserve their count-basis contract.
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — a row whose lifetime ended by its own commit's end never exists at a commit boundary.
    pub(crate) fn retire_expired_births(&mut self) -> Result<crate::meter::Metered, RuntimeError> {
        let Some(now) = self.context.now else { return Ok(Default::default());
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.036.000.000#3 — an expired final image never grants a new recipient the row's id.
        // The tracker covers synced and unsynced source tables alike. Each
        // distinct entry prepaid its tree node, strings and this vector slot;
        // the rows-written ceiling bounds the commit-end walk.
        // A row that existed before the commit and ends it already expired is
        // retired too: accounted from the partitions it was in at the commit's
        // start, it revokes there and announces nothing to a partition it only
        // entered with its expiring image.
        let mut changed: Vec<(String, String)> = std::mem::take(&mut self.expiry_rows).into_iter().collect();
        if changed.is_empty() {
            return Ok(Default::default());
        }
        // The log's order must not depend on a map's iteration order.
        let order = self.schema.through_order();
        changed.sort_by_key(|(name,id)| {
            (std::cmp::Reverse(order.iter().position(|n| n == name)),id.clone(),
            )
        });
        // Server execution already reserved this bookkeeping in the source
        // writes. Device prediction instead runs the walk inside its separate
        // bounded physical-work meter: exhausting that allowance queues the
        // intent, never publishes a partial closure or refuses the write.
        let meter = if self.device_work_active { None } else {
            Some(std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter, crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::unbounded(), crate::meter::Cut::Abort)))
        };
        self.retiring = Some(Default::default());
        let retired = changed.iter().try_for_each(|(table_name, id)| {
            let Some(row) = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, table_name, id))? else { return Ok(());
            };

    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
        })
    }

    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    }

    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — a removed opening boundary cannot lower durable source retirement time.
        let now=if self.server {now.max(self.meta("time_audience_clock")?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))}else{now};
        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
        if programs.is_some() {
            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
            }
            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
        }
        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);
        self.check_ownership()?;
        let sql = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
        // Low-level preparation may precede a writer-lock wait. Recheck all
        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.
        Self::check_version(&sql)?;
        let before = identity(&sql)?;
        if before != self.identity { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("another engine activated this store; reopen it before activating source".into())); }
        check(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — physical upgrades roll back with adoption.
        // Rollback restores schema/rows and freelist count. It may leave
        // changed free-page contents or journal/WAL residue, not committed data.
        Self::initialize_schema(&sql)?;
        let now=if self.server {
            now.max(sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='time_audience_clock'",[],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|s|s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN))
        }else{now};
        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }
        index_key_guards(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — reuse an admitted key bound, never assume a legacy store was checked.
        // Physical INSERT/UPDATE triggers invalidate the proof on oversized keys,
        // including direct SQL. Every activation key
        // below is checked too. Bind the proof to the predecessor so an older
        // binary's adoption invalidates it; device installation always scans.
        let key_proof = before.1.as_ref().map(|hash| format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES));
        let validated: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let keys_validated = programs.is_some() && key_proof.is_some() && validated == key_proof;
        #[cfg(test)]
        let keys_validated = keys_validated && !self.full_adoption;
        // One bounded result, no row images. Fresh stores have no schema;
        // an explicit device reset discards the old entries in this transaction.
        if before.0.is_some() && !reset && !keys_validated {
            let violation: Option<(String, i64)> = sql.query_row(
                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
            ).optional()?;
            if let Some((name, count)) = violation {
                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));
                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(table, index, count as u64));
            }
        }
        report.phase("key validation", &mut phase);
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#6-devices-and-partitions — executable native content changes the existing semantic generation.
        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let activation_hash = programs.map(|programs| match &native {Some(native)=>digest(&(schema,programs,native)),None=>digest(&(schema,programs))});
        let schema_changed = before.0.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash);
        let mut changed = schema_changed || activation_hash.as_ref().is_some_and(|hash| before.1.as_ref() != Some(hash));
        // The address function is part of the semantic generation even when
        // the authored schema and programs are byte-identical. Devices reset
        // with that generation; the server rebuilds old target rows below.
        let predecessor_has_aggregate = sql.query_row(
            "SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')",
            [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
        ).optional()?.map(|text| serde_json::from_str::<Schema>(&text)
            .map_err(|error| RuntimeError::decode(error, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?
            .is_some_and(|previous| previous.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
        let maintained_identity_upgrade = programs.is_some()
            && predecessor_has_aggregate
            && sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key=?1", [crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref()
                != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
        changed |= maintained_identity_upgrade;
        let mut catalog: BTreeMap<String, i64> = sql.prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?
            .query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        // Repeat source-count admission under the writer lock, before any candidate writes.
        let preflight_catalog = catalog.clone();
        let (launches, sql) = {
            let preflight = Tx { activation_scan: None, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
                server: self.server,
                catalog: &preflight_catalog };
            let launches = if programs.is_some() { super::timeline_launch::preflight(&preflight, schema, now, allow_launch)? } else { Vec::new() };
            let Tx { tx: sql, .. } = preflight;
            (launches, sql)
        };
        let mut next = catalog.values().copied().max().unwrap_or(0) + 1;
        for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {
            for full in std::iter::once(name.clone()).chain(table.indexes.keys().map(|index| format!("{name}.{index}"))) {
                if !catalog.contains_key(&full) {
                    sql.execute("INSERT INTO _catalog(name, num) VALUES (?1, ?2)", params![full, next])?;
                    catalog.insert(full, next); next += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        let activation_scan = programs.map(|_| Default::default());
        #[cfg(test)]
        let activation_scan = if self.full_adoption { None } else { activation_scan };
        let tx = Tx { activation_scan, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
            server: self.server,
            catalog: &catalog };
        let now = if schema.tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
            tx.pin_time_audience_clock(now)?
        } else {
            now
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#12.1 — occupancy belongs to successful activation,
        // never opening a legacy store that may subsequently refuse adoption.
        let cap_counts = crate::rows::caps::required(schema) || tx.meta("cap_counts_schema")?.is_some();
        if cap_counts {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _cap_counts (t INTEGER NOT NULL, k BLOB NOT NULL, n INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(n >= 0), PRIMARY KEY(t, k)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _cap_counts;")?; }
        }
        if schema.tables.values().any(|t| !t.sequences.is_empty()) {
            tx.ensure_sequence_table()?;
        }
        if reset {
            tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM r; DELETE FROM i;")?;
            if tx.has_sequence_table()? { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _sequences;")?; }

pub fn serve_release(options: &Options) -> Result<(), String> {
    let requested_port = crate::port_flag::parse("serve", options.flags.get("port").map(String::as_str))?;
    let bind = release_bind(options)?;
    let capacity = crate::host_limits::Capacity::from_options(&options.flags, |name| {
        std::env::var(name).ok()
    })?;
    let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let owner = crate::store_owner::host(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))?;
    owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose"));
    let clock_offset = options.flags.get("clock-offset").map(|text| crate::dev_clock::offset(text).map_err(|e| e.to_string())).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
    if options.flags.contains_key("clock-offset") {
        let store = owner.open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release().to_string()); }
    }
    let current = current(&dir, &owner)?.ok_or("no current release; deploy first")?;
    let release = Project {
        root: dir.join("releases").join(current.to_string())
            .canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    };
    crate::release_files::link_store(&release.root)?;
    let (port, listener) = crate::port_flag::listen("serve", requested_port, bind, None)?;
    let worker = options
        .flags
        .get("worker")
        .map(|path| {
            Path::new(path)
                .canonicalize()
                .map_err(|e| format!("--worker {path}: {e}"))
        })
        .transpose()?;
    crate::serve::serve(
        release,
        port,
        crate::serve::Mode::Release {
            clock_offset,
            expected: current,
            worker,
            owner: std::sync::Arc::new(owner),
        },
        &bind.to_string(),
        listener,

 succeeded in 0ms:
//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, producing_engine, respond_with_cors};
use snapback4_core::{error::Refusal, identity::{self, Session}};
use std::{io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, net::TcpStream, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use tiny_http::{Method, Request};

pub(crate) const ENDPOINT: &str = "/dev/token";
#[path = "serve_token_protocol.rs"]
mod protocol;
use protocol::{Mint, CLIENT, SERVER, PROOF};
pub(crate) use protocol::Authority;
const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;
const SECRET_LEN: usize = 64;

fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    path.push("-token");
    path.into()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        file.set_len(0)?;
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
        }
        let mut secret = String::new();
        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
        }
        Ok(secret)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
}

pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
        },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let Some(server) = server else {
        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
    };
    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let result = {
        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
            let now = if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment")?.as_deref() == Some("staging") { shared.now() } else { snapback4_core::now_ms() };
            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, now, mint.days)
        })
    };
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
        Err(error) => {
            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
            json!({"denied": why})
        }
    };
    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
}

fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
    let mut response = String::new();
    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
    }
    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
        }
        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
    }
    let session = &reply["session"];
    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
    }
    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
}

#[test]
fn a_new_named_declaration_transforms_existing_historical_inputs() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";

}

 }

#[test]
fn reentry_restores_shared_snapshots_for_arguments_and_point_reads() {
 let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let twice=old.to_owned()+"backfill items twice(n: int):\n  {n:n * 2}\n";
 for later in [false,true] {
  let copied=twice.replace("  n: int","  copied: int\n  lookedUp: int\n  n: int")+"backfill items copy(n: int, id: items):\n  {copied:n, lookedUp:(items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND).n}\n";
  let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"items",json!({"id":"a","n":3}));
  if later {adopt(&mut e,&twice,2).unwrap();}
  adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();let expected=if later {6}else{3};
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));
  adopt(&mut e,"query empty():\n  return true\n",4).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,&copied,3).unwrap();
  assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","n"),Value::Int(6));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","copied"),Value::Int(expected));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"items","a","lookedUp"),Value::Int(expected));
 }
}

fn partial_table_reentry_restores_carried_declarations_in_the_same_cohort() {

//! Release activation extends the existing engine transaction and generation.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — CAS includes every activation, not only semantic changes.
use super::*;
use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ReleaseActivation {
    pub release: u64,
    pub expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    pub expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    pub restore_dormant: bool,
    pub native_digest: Option<String>,
    pub operation: String,
}
impl ReleaseActivation {
    pub fn new(release: u64, expected_previous: Option<u64>) -> Self {
        Self {
            release,
            expected_previous,
            expected_revision: None,
            restore_dormant: true,
            native_digest: None,
            operation: "activate".into(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn check(&self, conn: &rusqlite::Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let actual: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        if actual
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok() != self.expected_previous)
        {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("current release changed; run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry the selected release".into()));
        }
        let revision: Option<String> = conn
            .query_row(
                "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_revision'",
                [],
                |r| r.get(0),
            )
            .optional()?;
        let revision = revision.and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        if self.expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != revision) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("activation revision changed (including rollback/equal-content activation); run `snapback4 releases <dir>` and retry".into()));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Engine {
    pub fn new_release(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_with_feed_adoption(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            release,
            expected_previous,
            false,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_with_feed_adoption(
        store: Store,
        schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        release: u64,
        expected_previous: Option<u64>,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            &ReleaseActivation::new(release, expected_previous),
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared(
        store: Store, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        Self::new_release_prepared_at(store,schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn new_release_prepared_at(
        mut store: Store,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule: timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral: std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(
                metadata.store_id,
                generation,
                &schema,
            )),
            store,
            schema,
            programs,
            generation,
            expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(),
            dev_effects: false,
            ceilings: Ceilings::default(),
            entropy: crate::ids::seed(),
            minter: crate::ids::Mint::default(),
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native: None,
            #[cfg(feature = "native")]
            native_logs: native::Logs::default(),
            presence: None,
        })
    }
    /// No replacement engine, post-commit SQL read, or fallible ephemeral swap.
    pub fn adopt_release(
        &mut self, schema: Schema, programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation, allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.adopt_release_at(schema,programs,options,allow_feed_adoption,crate::now_ms())
    }
    pub fn adopt_release_at(
        &mut self,
        mut schema: Schema,
        programs: impl IntoIterator<Item = Program>,
        options: &ReleaseActivation,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
        now: i64,
    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        prepare(&mut schema)?;
        let programs = programs.into_iter().map(|p| (p.name.clone(), p)).collect();
        drop(self.ephemeral.lock()?);
        let (generation, metadata) = Self::activate(
            &mut self.store,
            &schema,
            &programs,
            Some(options),
            now,
            allow_feed_adoption,
            None,
            None,
        )?;
        self.store.exclude_ephemeral(&schema);
        self.timeline_schedule = timeline::Schedule::default();
        self.schema = schema;
        self.programs = programs;
        self.generation = generation;
        self.expiry_after.clear();
        self.dev_effects = false;
        self.ephemeral
            .rebind_committed(metadata.store_id, generation, &self.schema);
        Ok(generation)
    }
    #[cfg(feature = "native")]
    pub fn set_prepared_native(&mut self, module: Option<std::sync::Arc<native::Module>>) {
        self.native = module;
    }
}

fn prepare(schema: &mut Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    columns::validate_before_prepare(schema)?;
    schema.check_row_view_audiences()?;
    schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    schema.prepare_delivery_lookup();
    if let Some(issue) = crate::schema::validate(schema).first() {
        return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
            "{}: {}",
            issue.path, issue.message
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Private filesystem-owner control transport; never an HTTP application door.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — the host remains the only database writer.
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
#[cfg(unix)]
use std::os::unix::{
    ffi::OsStrExt,
    fs::{DirBuilderExt, FileTypeExt, MetadataExt, OpenOptionsExt, PermissionsExt},
    net::{UnixListener, UnixStream},
};
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{self, Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

const INPUT: usize = 4096;
const OUTPUT: usize = 64 * 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "command", rename_all = "kebab-case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub(crate) enum Command {
    Status {
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<String>,
        event: Option<u64>,
    },
    Activate {
        release: u64,
        expected_release: Option<u64>,
        expected_revision: u64,
        digest: String,
        rollback: bool,
        restore_dormant: bool,
        allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    },
    Prune {
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: u64,
    },
}
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Envelope {
    identity: String,
    command: Command,
}

#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) fn identity(store: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    let path = store.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let m = snapback4_core::private_fs::database_metadata(&path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .ok_or("store disappeared")?;
    Ok(crate::native::digest(
        &serde_json::to_vec(&(path.as_os_str().as_bytes(), m.dev(), m.ino())).unwrap(),
    ))
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn peer(stream: &UnixStream) -> io::Result<()> {
    use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    let uid = {
        let mut credentials = std::mem::MaybeUninit::<libc::ucred>::uninit();
        let mut size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() as libc::socklen_t;
        if unsafe {
            libc::getsockopt(
                stream.as_raw_fd(),
                libc::SOL_SOCKET,
                libc::SO_PEERCRED,
                credentials.as_mut_ptr().cast(),
                &mut size,
            )
        } != 0
        {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        if size as usize != std::mem::size_of::<libc::ucred>() {
            return Err(io::Error::other("invalid peer credentials"));
        }
        unsafe { credentials.assume_init() }.uid
    };
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    let uid = {
        let (mut uid, mut gid) = (0, 0);
        if unsafe { libc::getpeereid(stream.as_raw_fd(), &mut uid, &mut gid) } != 0 {
            return Err(io::Error::last_os_error());
        }
        uid
    };
    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos")))]
    return Err(io::Error::other(
        "release control peer credentials unsupported on this Unix host",
    ));
    #[cfg(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "macos"))]
    if uid != unsafe { libc::geteuid() } {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control requires the same filesystem owner",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn socket_entry(path: &Path) -> io::Result<fs::Metadata> {
    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
    if !m.file_type().is_socket()
        || m.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
        || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
    {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control must be a same-owner 0600 socket, never a symlink or regular file",
        ));
    }
    Ok(m)
}

impl Shared {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — current adopted auth policy closes test authority, independently of dev mode.
    pub(crate) fn dev_doors(&self) -> bool {
        if !self.personas { return false; }
        let bound = lock(&self.bound);
        self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
    }
    // Revalidate under the writer: neither a slow body nor an adoption queue
    // may carry fixture authority into a newly password-only schema.
    pub(crate) fn dev_authority(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
        if self.personas && (self.unsafe_dev_doors || !(engine.schema().auth.password && !engine.schema().auth.guests)) { Ok(()) }
        else { Err(dev_doors::closed()) }
    }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 { lock(&self.clock).now().max(self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire)) }
    pub(crate) fn refresh_time_audience_clock(&self, engine: &Engine, now: i64) {
        if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
            let durable = engine.store().meta("time_audience_clock").ok().flatten()
                .and_then(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(i64::MIN);
            self.time_audience_clock.fetch_max(now.max(durable), Ordering::AcqRel);
        } else {
            self.time_audience_clock.store(i64::MIN, Ordering::Release);
        }
    }
    fn advance_time_audiences(&self) -> Result<i64, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        let requested = lock(&self.clock).now();
        // Non-timed reader traffic must retain its independent reader lane,
        // including while adoption or a mutation deliberately holds the writer.
        if self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire) == i64::MIN { return Ok(requested); }
        let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
        let before = engine.seq()?;
        let now = engine.advance_time_audiences_at(requested)?;
        self.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, now);
        if engine.seq()? != before { self.durable_committed(&engine); }
        Ok(now)
    }
    fn next_time_audience(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        if self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire) == i64::MIN { return Ok(None); }
        lock(&self.engine).next_time_audience_at()
    }
    fn time_audience_delay(&self, at: i64) -> Duration {
        let raw = lock(&self.clock).now();
        let floor = self.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire);
        Duration::from_millis(if at <= floor { 0 } else { at.saturating_sub(raw).max(0) }.try_into().unwrap_or(u64::MAX))
    }
    pub(crate) fn worker_owner(&self) -> Option<Arc<std::fs::File>> { self.owner.as_ref().map(|owner| owner.worker_lock()) }
}

#[path = "serve_trace.rs"]
mod trace;

#[path = "serve_readers.rs"]
mod query_readers;
use query_readers::QueryReaders;

/// A panicking request must not turn one poisoned process mutex into a
/// permanent server-wide panic loop. The guarded value is retained, the
/// poison marker is cleared, and the next request can make progress.
pub(crate) fn lock<T: Lockable>(mutex: &T) -> T::Guard<'_> {
    mutex.recover_lock()
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct AssetTransfers {
    active: Mutex<BTreeSet<(String, String)>>,
    available: Condvar,
}

impl AssetTransfers {
    pub(crate) fn enter<'a>(&'a self, asset: &str, principal: &str) -> AssetTransfer<'a> {
        let key = (asset.to_owned(), principal.to_owned());
        let mut active = lock(&self.active);
        while active.contains(&key) {
            active = self.available.wait(active).unwrap_or_else(|poisoned| poisoned.into_inner());
        }
        active.insert(key.clone());
        AssetTransfer { transfers: self, key }
    }
}

pub(crate) struct AssetTransfer<'a> {
    transfers: &'a AssetTransfers,
    key: (String, String),
}

impl Drop for AssetTransfer<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        lock(&self.transfers.active).remove(&self.key);
        self.transfers.available.notify_all();
    }
}

    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.
        let generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
        let status = lock(&shared.adoption);
        let now = shared.now();
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Options {
        respond_empty(request, 204);
        return;
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    }
    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
    }
    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow
    // upload cannot retain the authority held at its start.
    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside
    // the SQLite writer transaction that closes the live session family.
    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")
        || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && path.ends_with("/cancel"))
        || matches!(path.as_str(), "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout-all")) {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            // No authenticated viewer: metadata cannot disclose a store head.
            if path.starts_with("/q/") { return respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/auth/revoke" || path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && !path.ends_with("/cancel"))) {
        if let Err(refusal) = service_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Get && matches!(path.as_str(), "/" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/changes") {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    let _upload_permit = if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
        match crate::assets::admit(shared) {
            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
            None => {
                let mut refusal = Refusal::new("E_RATE_LIMIT", "auth", "too many concurrent uploads; retry shortly");
                refusal.retryable = true;
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
            }
        }
    } else { None };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();

//! Host startup; request scheduling remains in serve.rs.
use super::*;
pub fn serve(
    project: Project,
    port: u16,
    mut mode: Mode,
    bind: &str,
    inherited: Option<std::net::TcpListener>,
    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let capacity = capacity.fit()?;
    crate::host_limits::install(capacity);
    let mut source_stamp = project.sources_stamp();
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        let verbose = !matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: false, .. });
        let (reports, hidden) = crate::verbs::problem_reports(&bound, true, verbose);
        let shown = reports.len();
        for report in reports {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{report}");
        }
        if hidden > 0 {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 dev: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"
            );
        }

            "{shown} problem{} in snapback/",
            if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
        ));
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — replay suppresses new effects and holds schedule admission; ordinary holds keep their separate meanings.

        Mode::Dev {
            hold_effects: true,
            ..
        }
    );

                ..
            }
        );

    67	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(definition) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;

    90	pub(super) fn transition(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,now:i64,report:&mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{

   100	            }
   101	        }}
   102	    }
   103	    // A name is immutable within a continuous lineage. Replacing a transform
   104	    // deliberately goes through an old-source/bridge transition first.
   105	    if let Some(old)=previous {for f in &next.backfills {if old.backfills.iter().any(|p|p.table==f.table && p.name==f.name && p.key()!=f.key()) {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} changed; use a new name in a bridge release rather than replacing historical meaning",f.table,f.name)));}}}

   114	            return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} changed historical meaning; use a new name in a bridge release",old.table,old.name)));
   115	        }

   122	    for fill in definitions.iter().rev().filter(|f|!next_keys.contains(&f.key())) {
