   125	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
   126	            let Some(mut e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&key)? else {
   127	                let pruned:Option<(bool,String)>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT active,retained_outputs FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3",params![t,id,key],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).optional()?;
   128	                let unavailable=if let Some((active,retained))=pruned {if active {fill.outputs.clone()}else{decode::<BTreeSet<String>>(&retained)?}}else{BTreeSet::new()};
   129	                if unavailable.iter().any(|c|table.columns.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,"rollback preimage was explicitly pruned; select a bridge preserving current data or restore a backup");}
   130	                return Ok(())
   131	            };
   132	            if !e.active && e.retained.is_empty() {return Ok(())}
   133	            let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid stored row"))};
   134	            let mut changed=false;
   135	            for c in &fill.outputs {
   136	                if !e.active && !e.retained.contains(c){continue;}
   137	                if !table.columns.contains_key(c){e.retained.insert(c.clone());continue;}
   138	
   139	                let current=fields.get(c);let before=e.before.get(c);let after=e.after.get(c);
   140	                if current!=after && current!=before {bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} changed after backfill {}; keep a bridge and repair through an authorized mutation before rollback",fill.table,c,fill.name));return Ok(())}
   141	                if let Some(value)=before {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{
   142	                    fields.remove(c);
   143	                    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_unprovided VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![t,id,c])?;
   144	                }
   145	                e.retained.remove(c);
   146	                changed=true;
   147	            }
   148	            if changed {tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;count+=1;}
   149	            save(tx,t,id,&key,&e,false)
   150	        })?;
   151	        if bad.count>0 {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {} rollback: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
   152	        if count>0 {report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} reversed on {count} {}",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1 {"row"}else{"rows"}));tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;}
   153	    }
   154	    let old_keys:BTreeSet<_>=previous.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.backfills.iter().map(Backfill::key)).collect();
   155	    let restored_tables:BTreeSet<i64>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT n.t FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=n.t AND r.id=n.id)")?.query_map([],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   156	    let entering:Vec<_>=next.backfills.iter().filter(|f|!old_keys.contains(&f.key()) || tx.num(&f.table).is_ok_and(|t|restored_tables.contains(&t))).collect();
   157	    if entering.is_empty(){return Ok(())}
   158	    let populated = entering.iter().try_fold(false, |any,fill| -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
   159	        Ok(any || tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1)",[tx.num(&fill.table)?],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?)
   160	    })?;
   161	    if !populated {return Ok(())}
   162	    let mut source=previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();source.tables=sources.clone();source.backfills.clear();
   163	    // Point lookup indexes are rebuilt against the assembled source images,
   164	    // including restored rows, before any output patch is applied.
   165	    let mut reads=BTreeSet::new();
   166	    for f in &entering {collect_reads(&f.program.body,&mut reads);}
   167	    for (name,index) in &reads {
   168	        let wanted=next.table(name).and_then(|t|t.index(&index)).ok_or_else(||refusal("backfill lookup index absent"))?;
   169	        let old_table=source.tables.get_mut(name).ok_or_else(||refusal(format!("backfill lookup source {name} has no historical definition")))?;
   170	        if !wanted.is_plain() || wanted.columns().iter().any(|c|c!="id" && !old_table.columns.contains_key(c)) {return Err(refusal(format!("backfill lookup {name}.{index} requires predecessor fields; use an indexed bridge")))}
   171	        old_table.indexes.insert(index.clone(),wanted.clone());
   172	    }
   173	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TEMP TABLE _backfill_pending(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,patch TEXT NOT NULL,evidence TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,definition)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   174	    tx.conn().execute_batch("CREATE TEMP TABLE _backfill_originals(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
   175	    let entering_keys:BTreeSet<_>=entering.iter().map(|f|f.key()).collect();
   176	    let mut epochs=BTreeSet::from([None]);
   177	    for key in &entering_keys {
   178	        let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT epoch FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE definition=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id)")?;
   179	        for epoch in q.query_map([key],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0))? {epochs.insert(Some(epoch?));}
   180	    }
   181	    let fresh_epoch=tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0).saturating_add(1);
   182	    for epoch in epochs {
   183	        restore_snapshot(tx)?;
   184	        mask_unprovided(tx)?;
   185	        if let Some(epoch)=epoch {
   186	            replay_earlier_cohorts(tx,epoch)?;
   187	            historical_snapshot(tx,epoch,&next_keys)?;
   188	            inactive_snapshot(tx,previous,&entering_keys,epoch)?;
   189	        }
   190	        rebuild_reads(tx,&source,&reads)?;
   191	    for fill in &entering {
   192	
   193	        let t=tx.num(&fill.table)?;let key=fill.key();let table=&next.tables[&fill.table];let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();let mut count=0;
   194	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,mut row|{
   195	            if old_keys.contains(&key) && !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {return Ok(())}
   196	            let row_source=row_source(tx,t,id,sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   197	            if let Some(old)=row_source.as_deref() {if let Some(c)=fill.outputs.iter().find(|c|source_owned(old,c)) {
   198	                bad.add(id,&format!("output {c} is a historical lifetime or engine field; preserve its source meaning in a bridge"));return Ok(())
   199	            }}
   200	            let prior=evidence(tx,t,id,&key)?;
   201	            if prior.as_ref().map(|e|e.epoch)!=epoch {return Ok(())}
   202	            let original:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM temp._backfill_originals WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   203	            let current:Value=if let Some(text)=original {decode(&text)?}else{row.clone()};
   204	            if prior.is_none() && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition=?3)",params![t,id,key],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   205	                bad.add(id,"historical backfill evidence was explicitly pruned; cannot safely re-enter this declaration; use a new bridge or restore a backup");return Ok(())
   206	            }
   207	            for c in fill.inputs.keys() {
   208	                if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   209	                    bad.add(id,&format!("historical reference {}.{c} lost its parent lifetime",fill.table));return Ok(())
   210	                }
   211	            }
   212	            let physically_absent=absence(tx,t,id,"")?;
   213	            let native=absence(tx,t,id,&format!("$native:{key}"))?;
   214	            if prior.is_none() && !native.is_empty() {
   215	                let native_inputs=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&current,fill.inputs.keys().filter(|c|!fill.outputs.contains(*c)).map(String::as_str),row_source.as_deref())?;
   216	                if fill.inputs.iter().any(|(c,kind)|!fill.outputs.contains(c) && !physically_absent.contains(c) && crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,native_inputs.get(c)).is_err()) {
   217	                    bad.add(id,"native image has an invalid present historical input; use a typed bridge");return Ok(())
   218	                }
   219	                let mut conflict=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?;
   220	                let others:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   221	                for other in others {if let Some(e)=evidence(tx,t,id,&other)? {
   222	                    for c in fill.outputs.intersection(&e.after.keys().cloned().collect()) {
   223	                        if e.active || current.field(c).ok()!=e.before.get(c) {conflict=true;}
   224	                    }
   225	                }}
   226	                if conflict {
   227	                    bad.add(id,"native output also has a different transformation's history; resolve its intended view in a bridge");return Ok(())
   228	                }
   229	                let complete=fill.outputs.iter().all(|c|current.field(c).is_ok() && validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,current.field(c).unwrap()).is_ok());
   230	                let fingerprint=crate::value::digest(&native_inputs);
   231	                if !complete {bad.add(id,"native backfill output image is missing or was explicitly pruned; use a bridge or restore a backup");return Ok(())}
   232	                if !native.contains(&fingerprint) {bad.add(id,"native backfill inputs changed after row creation; preserve the authored output in a bridge before re-entry");return Ok(())}
   233	                return Ok(())
   234	            }
   235	            let mut original_absence=prior.as_ref().map_or_else(||physically_absent.intersection(&fill.inputs.keys().cloned().collect()).cloned().collect(),|e|e.absent.clone());
   236	            for c in fill.inputs.keys() {if (row.field(c).is_err() || row.field(c)==Ok(&Value::Null)) && tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,c],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {original_absence.insert(c.clone());}}
   237	            // Resolve retained inputs before source defaults can synthesize null
   238	            // for a hidden optional column with a provided archived cell.
   239	            let retained=native_inputs(tx,t,id,&row,fill.inputs.keys().map(String::as_str),row_source.as_deref())?;
   240	            if let Some(table)=row_source.as_deref(){materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);}
   241	            let mut args=BTreeMap::new();
   242	            for (c,kind) in &fill.inputs {
   243	                let value=retained.get(c).cloned();
   244	                if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(&fill.table,c,kind,value.as_ref()).is_err() {
   245	                    bad.add(id,&format!("historical input {}.{c} is absent or does not fit {kind}; use a typed bridge to repair it",fill.table));return Ok(())
   246	                }
   247	                if let Some(prior)=&prior {if prior.inputs.field(c).ok()==value.as_ref() {
   248	                    let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   249	                    if invalid {bad.add(id,&format!("historical backfill reference {c} lost its parent lifetime"));return Ok(())}
   250	                }}
   251	                args.insert(c.clone(),value.unwrap_or(Value::Null));
   252	            }
   253	            let inputs=Value::Row(args.clone().into());
   254	            let mut context=Context::system().at(now);context.args=args;
   255	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,context,Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),0);
   256	            let patch=match runner.run(&fill.program) {Ok(Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f))=>f.into_iter().collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),Ok(_)=>{bad.add(id,"backfill did not return an object");return Ok(())},Err(e)=>{bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}};
   257	            if runner.read_cut().is_some() {bad.add(id,"historical lookup did not complete; reduce per-row lookup work before adoption");return Ok(())}
   258	            if patch.keys().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()!=fill.outputs {return Err(refusal("backfill returned undeclared output fields"))}
   259	            let fields=current.as_object()?;let mut before=BTreeMap::new();let mut desired=patch.clone();
   260	            let mut preimage_meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   261	            for (c,value) in &patch {
   262	                if let Err(e)=validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,value) {bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}
   263	                if let Some(kind)=row_source.as_deref().and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)) {if protected(kind) && prior.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.before.contains_key(c)) {bad.add(id,&format!("cannot overwrite historical reference or asset {}.{c}; derive into a new column",fill.table));return Ok(())}}
   264	                let transformed=prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.active || e.retained.contains(c));
   265	                if transformed {
   266	                    if let Some(value)=prior.as_ref().and_then(|e|e.before.get(c)){before.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}
   267	                } else if row_source.as_deref().is_some_and(|t|t.columns.contains_key(c)) {
   268	                    if let Some(value)=source_value(tx,t,id,&current,c,row_source.as_deref(),&mut preimage_meter)? {before.insert(c.clone(),value);}
   269	                }
   270	                if let Some(prior)=&prior {
   271	                    let deps_same=prior.inputs==inputs && prior.after==patch;
   272	                    let current=fields.get(c);
   273	                    let original=prior.before.get(c);
   274	                    if !transformed && prior.before.contains_key(c) && before.get(c)!=original && before.get(c)!=prior.after.get(c) {
   275	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} changed while backfill {} was absent; keep a bridge and resolve the conflict",fill.table,c,fill.name));return Ok(())
   276	                    }
   277	                    if deps_same && transformed && current.is_some() {
   278	                        // Added columns retain edits under v1's dormant storage.
   279	                        desired.insert(c.clone(),current.unwrap().clone());
   280	                    } else if transformed && !deps_same && current!=prior.after.get(c) && current!=original {
   281	                        bad.add(id,&format!("{}.{} was edited and its historical inputs changed; keep a bridge and resolve the conflict",fill.table,c));return Ok(())
   282	                    }
   283	                }
   284	            }
   285	            for(c,value)in &desired {if let Err(e)=validate_output(&fill.table,table,c,value){bad.add(id,&e.to_string());return Ok(())}}
   286	            let e=Evidence{retained:BTreeSet::new(),absent:original_absence,epoch:epoch.unwrap_or(fresh_epoch),active:true,inputs,before,after:patch};
   287	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO temp._backfill_pending VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5)",params![t,id,key,canonical_json(&desired),canonical_json(&e)])?;count+=1;
   288	            Ok(())
   289	        })?;
   290	        if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("backfill {} {}: {}",fill.table,fill.name,bad.describe())))}
   291	        if count>0{report.backfills.push(format!("{} {} transformed {count} {}",fill.table,fill.name,if count==1 {"row"}else{"rows"}));}
   292	    }
   293	    }
   294	    restore_snapshot(tx)?;
   295	    // Stream patches; avoid retaining the whole transformed population in RAM.
   296	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition,patch,evidence FROM temp._backfill_pending ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   297	    let mut rows=stmt.query([])?;
   298	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   299	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;
   300	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;let e:Evidence=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(4)?)?;
   301	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   302	        if let Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=&mut row {f.extend(patch);}
   303	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   304	        let prior=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?;
   305	        save(tx,t,&id,&key,&e,true)?;
   306	        for c in e.after.keys() {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND definition='' AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c])?;}
   307	        let fill=next.backfills.iter().find(|f|f.key()==key).ok_or_else(||refusal("missing historical declaration"))?;
   308	        let row_source=row_source(tx,t,&id,sources.get(&fill.table))?;
   309	        for(c,kind)in &fill.inputs {
   310	            // A generic id argument cannot erase the source column's lifetime.
   311	            let kind=row_source.as_deref().and_then(|t|t.columns.get(c)).filter(|k|protected(k)).unwrap_or(kind);
   312	            if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c) {
   313	                let binding=input_ref(&key,c);
   314	                let unchanged=prior.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.inputs.field(c).ok()==Some(value));
   315	                capture_input_refs(tx,t,&id,c,&binding,kind,value,unchanged)?;
   316	            }
   317	        }
   318	    }
   319	    drop(rows);drop(stmt);
   320	    tx.conn().execute_batch("DROP TABLE temp._backfill_pending; DROP TABLE temp._backfill_originals;")?;
   321	    // The source-index rebuild and every transformed dependency require full
   322	    // candidate key/cap/maintain validation, even when its declaration is equal.
   323	    tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;
   324	    Ok(())
   325	}
   326	fn collect_reads(body:&[crate::ir::Stmt],out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){
   327	    use crate::ir::{Expr,Stmt};
   328	    fn expr(e:&Expr,out:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>){match e{
   329	        Expr::Scan(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for k in &r.prefix{if let Some(e)=k.scalar(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(range)=&r.range {for e in [&range.gt,&range.gte,&range.lt,&range.lte].into_iter().flatten(){expr(e,out);}}if let Some(e)=&r.filter{expr(e,out);}},
   330	        Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>{out.insert((r.table.clone(),r.index.clone()));for e in &r.key{expr(e,out);}},
   331	        Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base,out),Expr::Array(es)|Expr::Builtin{args:es,..}=>{for e in es{expr(e,out);}},
   332	        Expr::Object(fs)=>{for e in fs.values(){expr(e,out);}},Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{expr(base,out);for e in fields.values(){expr(e,out);}},
   333	        Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{expr(left,out);expr(right,out);},Expr::Block{body,value}=>{collect_reads(body,out);expr(value,out);},_=>()
   334	    }}
   335	    for s in body{match s{Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value,out),Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>expr(condition,out),Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{expr(condition,out);collect_reads(then_body,out);collect_reads(else_body,out);},_=>()}}
   336	}
   337	
   338	// Prune forgets historical values, retaining only the fact that exact reversal
   339	// is no longer available. No tombstone can be queried by an app.
   340	pub(super) fn prune(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   341	    if present(tx)? {tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_evidence.t AND r.id=_backfill_evidence.id); DELETE FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_pruned.t AND r.id=_backfill_pruned.id); INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_pruned(t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs) SELECT t,id,definition,active,retained_outputs FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_evidence; DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE (definition!='' AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%') OR NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=_backfill_absence.t AND r.id=_backfill_absence.id);")?;}
   342	    Ok(())
   343	}
   344	pub(super) fn inventory_sql(conn:&Connection)->Result<&'static str,RuntimeError>{
   345	    let exists:bool=conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_backfill_evidence')",[],|r|r.get(0))?;
   346	    Ok(if exists {" UNION ALL SELECT c.name||'.backfill#'||substr(e.definition,1,12),'backfill',c.name,e.definition FROM _backfill_evidence e JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=e.t GROUP BY e.t,e.definition "}else{""})
   347	}
   348	
   349	pub(crate) fn has_evidence(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{present(tx)}
   350	pub(super) fn columns(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema)->Result<BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>,RuntimeError>{
   351	    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
   352	    for f in &next.backfills {out.entry(f.table.clone()).or_default().extend(f.outputs.iter().cloned());}
   353	    if present(tx)? {
   354	        let mut statement=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition FROM _backfill_definitions WHERE digest IN (SELECT definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]' UNION SELECT definition FROM _backfill_pruned WHERE active=1 OR retained_outputs!='[]')")?;
   355	        for text in statement.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {
   356	            let f:Backfill=decode(&text?)?;out.entry(f.table).or_default().extend(f.outputs);
   357	        }
   358	    }
   359	    Ok(out)
   360	}
   361	fn validate_lookup(tx:&Tx<'_>,ti:i64,prefix:usize,table:&str,index:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   362	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT k,id FROM i WHERE ti=?1 ORDER BY k")?;let mut rows=stmt.query([ti])?;
   363	    let mut prior=None;let mut first=String::new();let mut duplicate=false;let mut bad=super::adopt_refusals::Samples::default();
   364	    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   365	        let key:Vec<u8>=row.get(0)?;let id:String=row.get(1)?;let mut group=crate::key::decode(&key).ok_or_else(||refusal("invalid historical lookup key"))?;group.truncate(prefix);
   366	        if group.contains(&Value::Null){continue;}
   367	        if prior.as_ref()==Some(&group) {
   368	            if !duplicate {bad.add(&first,"duplicate historical lookup key");duplicate=true;}
   369	            bad.add(&id,"duplicate historical lookup key");
   370	        }else{prior=Some(group);first=id;duplicate=false;}
   371	    }
   372	    if bad.count>0{return Err(refusal(format!("historical lookup {table}.{index} is not unique: {}; prepare a unique bridge before deriving values",bad.describe())))}
   373	    Ok(())
   374	}
   375	
   376	// Each original adoption cohort saw one snapshot. Re-entry restores that same
   377	// view across all its declarations; later cohorts read earlier cohorts' outputs.
   378	fn restore_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   379	    tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=(SELECT o.data FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_originals o WHERE o.t=r.t AND o.id=r.id)",[])?;Ok(())
   380	}
   381	// A reversed, originally absent cell must have the same meaning in explicit
   382	// arguments, point lookups and lookup indexes. Null is a temporary source-view
   383	// marker; the saved physical image is restored before committing any patch.
   384	fn mask_unprovided(tx:&Tx<'_>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   385	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT r.t,r.id,octet_length(r.data) FROM r JOIN temp._backfill_unprovided m ON m.t=r.t AND m.id=r.id ORDER BY r.t,r.id")?;
   386	    let mut rows=q.query([])?;
   387	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   388	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
   389	        let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);meter.heap((r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   390	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   391	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   392	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
   393	            let mut columns=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?;
   394	            for c in columns.query_map(params![t,id],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))? {fields.entry(c?).or_insert(Value::Null);}
   395	        }
   396	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   397	    }Ok(())
   398	}
   399	// Recorded later cohorts originally saw earlier releases' completed patches.
   400	// Replaying those older cohorts virtually preserves that ordering; fresh
   401	// declarations still run first against the untouched predecessor snapshot.
   402	fn replay_earlier_cohorts(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   403	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,octet_length(patch),patch FROM temp._backfill_pending WHERE CAST(json_extract(evidence,'$.epoch') AS INTEGER)<?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   404	    let mut rows=stmt.query([epoch])?;
   405	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   406	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;
   407	        let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   408	        let Some(bytes)=bytes else{continue};
   409	        let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.max(0) as u64).saturating_add(r.get::<_,i64>(2)?.max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   410	        let data:String=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0))?;let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;
   411	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   412	        let patch:BTreeMap<String,Value>=decode(&r.get::<_,String>(3)?)?;
   413	        if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {fields.extend(patch);}
   414	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   415	    }Ok(())
   416	}
   417	
   418	fn historical_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,epoch:i64,keys:&BTreeSet<String>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   419	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=1 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   420	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   421	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   422	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   423	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   424	        let Some(data)=data else {continue};
   425	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   426	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   427	        let fields=match &mut row {Value::Row(f)|Value::Map(f)=>f,_=>return Err(refusal("invalid historical row"))};
   428	        if let Value::Row(inputs)|Value::Map(inputs)=&e.inputs {for(c,value)in inputs {
   429	            if e.absent.contains(c) || !fields.contains_key(c) || fields.get(c)==Some(value) {
   430	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   431	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   432	                if e.absent.contains(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}else{fields.entry(c.clone()).or_insert(value.clone());}
   433	            }
   434	        }}
   435	        for c in e.after.keys() {if !e.before.contains_key(c) {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}}
   436	        for(c,value)in e.before {fields.insert(c,value);}
   437	        // Keep the receipt's omitted-input meaning separate from its later
   438	        // rollback output preimage/default.
   439	        for c in &e.absent {if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}}
   440	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   441	    } Ok(())
   442	}
   443	fn rebuild_reads(tx:&Tx<'_>,source:&Schema,reads:&BTreeSet<(String,String)>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   444	    for (name,index) in reads {
   445	        let old=&source.tables[name].indexes[index];let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;
   446	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   447	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,mut row|{
   448	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   449	            for key in index_keys(old,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   450	        })?;
   451	        if old.unique {validate_lookup(tx,ti,old.components.len(),name,index)?;}
   452	    } Ok(())
   453	}
   454	
   455	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#6-bakeoff-repairs — computed values obey the ordinary text minimum/format law before narrowing.
   456	fn validate_output(name:&str,table:&Table,column:&str,value:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   457	    crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name,column,&table.columns[column],Some(value))?;
   458	    for constraint in &table.constraints {if let crate::schema::Constraint::Text{field,min,format}=constraint {
   459	        if field==column {if let Value::String(text)=value {crate::engine::validate::validate_text(&format!("{name}.{column}"),text,*min,*format)?;}}
   460	    }}Ok(())
   461	}
   462	
   463	// Ordinary cell bindings can carry a newer transformed reference type than
   464	// the archived whole-row definition. Copy those exact lifetime bindings before
   465	// restoration consumes its ordinary ledger; same-value replay never rebinds.
   466	fn capture_input_refs(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,column:&str,binding:&str,kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType,value:&Value,unchanged:bool)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   467	    if !unchanged {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,binding])?;}
   468	    let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   469	    fn ids(value:&Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value {Value::Id(id)=>{out.insert(id.clone());},Value::Array(values)=>for value in values {ids(value,out)},_=>()}}
   470	    let mut values=BTreeSet::new();ids(value,&mut values);let mut known=BTreeSet::new();
   471	    let mut stmt=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT parent_t,parent_id,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3")?;
   472	    let mut rows=stmt.query(params![t,id,column])?;
   473	    while let Some(row)=rows.next()? {
   474	        let parent:String=row.get(1)?;if !values.contains(&parent){continue;}
   475	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",params![t,id,binding,row.get::<_,i64>(0)?,parent,row.get::<_,i64>(2)?])?;known.insert(parent);
   476	    }
   477	    super::dormant::capture_refs_preserving(tx,t,id,binding,kind,value,&known)
   478	}
   479	
   480	fn input_ref(key:&str,column:&str)->String {format!("$backfill:{key}:{column}")}
   481	
   482	// A prior transformation's retained added output is not its own old argument
   483	// on forward selection after rollback. Other declarations still see the new
   484	// predecessor snapshot rather than an old cohort's overwritten columns.
   485	fn inactive_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,_previous:Option<&Schema>,keys:&BTreeSet<String>,epoch:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   486	    let mut q=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT t,id,definition FROM _backfill_evidence WHERE active=0 AND epoch=?1 ORDER BY t,id,definition")?;
   487	    let mut rows=q.query([epoch])?;
   488	    while let Some(r)=rows.next()? {
   489	        let t:i64=r.get(0)?;let id:String=r.get(1)?;let key:String=r.get(2)?;if !keys.contains(&key){continue;}
   490	        let e=evidence(tx,t,&id,&key)?.unwrap();
   491	        if e.retained.is_empty() && e.absent.is_empty(){continue;}
   492	        let data:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;let Some(data)=data else{continue};
   493	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._backfill_originals SELECT t,id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id])?;
   494	        let mut row:Value=decode(&data)?;if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut row {
   495	            for c in &e.retained {fields.insert(c.clone(),e.before.get(c).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null));}
   496	            for c in &e.absent {
   497	                let invalid:bool=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND invalid=1)",params![t,id,input_ref(&key,c)],|r|r.get(0))?;
   498	                if invalid {return Err(refusal(format!("historical backfill reference {c} on {id} lost its parent lifetime")));}
   499	                if let Ok(value)=e.inputs.field(c){fields.insert(c.clone(),value.clone());}
   500	            }
   501	        }
   502	        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
   503	    }Ok(())
   504	}
   505	
   506	// Empty definition denotes currently omitted physical input cells; a definition
   507	// digest denotes absence at that receipt's adoption. Defaults may materialize
   508	// during retention without turning omitted inputs into authored values.
   509	pub(crate) fn prepare_inputs(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   510	    if next.backfills.is_empty() && previous.is_none_or(|s|s.backfills.is_empty()) && !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   511	    enable(tx)?;
   512	    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_active",[])?;
   513	    for fill in &next.backfills {tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _backfill_active VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![tx.num(&fill.table)?,fill.key(),canonical_json(&fill.inputs.keys().filter(|c|!fill.outputs.contains(*c)).collect::<Vec<_>>())])?;}
   514	    for fill in next.backfills.iter().chain(previous.into_iter().flat_map(|s|s.backfills.iter())) {
   515	        tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_definitions VALUES(?1,?2)",params![fill.key(),canonical_json(fill)])?;
   516	        for c in fill.inputs.keys() {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_inputs VALUES(?1,?2)",params![tx.num(&fill.table)?,c])?;}
   517	    }
   518	    let tables:Vec<i64>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT DISTINCT t FROM _backfill_inputs")?.query_map([],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   519	    for t in tables {
   520	        let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   521	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,row|{
   522	            for c in &columns {if row.field(c).is_err(){note_omission(tx,t,id,c)?;}}
   523	            Ok(())
   524	        })?;
   525	    } Ok(())
   526	}
   527	pub(super) fn observe_write(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,table:&Table,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   528	    if !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   529	    if old.is_none() {
   530	        let active:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT definition,input_names FROM _backfill_active WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   531	        for (key,names) in active {
   532	            let names:Vec<String>=decode(&names)?;
   533	            let fingerprint=input_fingerprint(&crate::schema::row_image(row,table),names.iter().map(String::as_str));
   534	            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_absence VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4)",params![t,id,format!("$native:{key}"),fingerprint])?;
   535	        }
   536	    }
   537	    let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   538	    if columns.is_empty(){return Ok(());}
   539	    let interpreted_old=old.map(|r|crate::schema::row_image(r,table));
   540	    let interpreted_new=crate::schema::row_image(row,table);
   541	    for c in columns {
   542	        let changed=interpreted_old.as_ref().is_none_or(|before|before.field(&c).ok()!=interpreted_new.field(&c).ok());
   543	        if changed {tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _backfill_absence WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3 AND definition NOT LIKE '$native:%' AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _backfill_evidence original JOIN _backfill_evidence owner ON owner.t=original.t AND owner.id=original.id AND owner.epoch=original.epoch JOIN json_each(owner.after_image) output ON output.key=?3 WHERE original.t=_backfill_absence.t AND original.id=_backfill_absence.id AND original.definition=_backfill_absence.definition AND owner.active=1)",params![t,id,c])?;}
   544	        if row.field(&c).is_err() {note_omission(tx,t,id,&c)?;}
   545	    } Ok(())
   546	}
   547	
   548	pub(super) fn record_omissions(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   549	    if !present(tx)? {return Ok(());}
   550	    let columns:Vec<String>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT column_name FROM _backfill_inputs WHERE t=?1")?.query_map([t],|r|r.get(0))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
   551	    for c in columns {if row.field(&c).is_err(){note_omission(tx,t,id,&c)?;}}Ok(())
   552	}
   553	
   554	fn input_fingerprint<'a>(row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>)->String {
   555	    let fields:BTreeMap<String,Value>=names.map(|c|(c.to_owned(),row.field(c).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null))).collect();
   556	    crate::value::digest(&fields)
   557	}
   558	
   559	fn note_omission(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,c:&str)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   560	    // A provided historical cell stays provided when merely moved out of the
   561	    // active projection. Already-recorded omission survives default freezing.
   562	    tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _backfill_absence SELECT ?1,?2,'',?3 WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,c])?;Ok(())
   563	}
   564	fn source_value(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value,column:&str,table:Option<&Table>,meter:&mut Meter)->Result<Option<Value>,RuntimeError>{
   565	    if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){meter.heap(value.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;return Ok(Some(value.clone()));}
   566	    if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM temp._backfill_unprovided WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3)",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {return Ok(None);}
   567	    let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   568	    meter.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0).max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
   569	    let text:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,column],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   570	    if let Some(text)=text{return Ok(Some(decode(&text)?));}
   571	    Ok(table.and_then(|table|{
   572	        if table.maintained.contains_key(column){Some(Value::Int(0))}
   573	        else if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind|kind.is_optional()){Some(Value::Null)}
   574	        else {table.defaults.get(column).and_then(|d|if let crate::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(v)=d {Some(v.clone())}else{None})}
   575	    }))
   576	}
   577	fn native_inputs<'a>(tx:&Tx<'_>,t:i64,id:&str,row:&Value,names:impl Iterator<Item=&'a str>,table:Option<&Table>)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Value>,RuntimeError>{
   578	    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
   579	    for c in names {out.insert(c.to_owned(),source_value(tx,t,id,row,c,table,&mut meter)?.unwrap_or(Value::Null));}Ok(out)
   580	}
   581	
   582	// @ref LLP 3000.039#3 — source expiry is an ordinary reference-action closure, never an unchecked physical delete.
   583	pub(super) fn retire_snapshot(tx:&Tx<'_>,sources:&BTreeMap<String,Table>,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
   584	    let mut source=previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();source.tables=sources.clone();source.backfills.clear();source.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
   585	    let names:Vec<String>=source.through_order().into_iter().rev().filter(|n|source.tables[n].ephemeral.is_none() && source.maintain_for(n).is_none()).collect();
   586	    let mut due=false;
   587	    for name in &names {
   588	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   589	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,row|{if !due {due=source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)?;}Ok(())})?;
   590	    }
   591	    if !due{return Ok(())}
   592	    // Restored sources have no live index entries. Every reference action,
   593	    // maintained dependency and retained-child probe uses this assembled view.
   594	    for name in &names {
   595	        let table=&source.tables[name];
   596	        for (index,definition) in &table.indexes {
   597	            let ti=tx.num(&format!("{name}.{index}"))?;tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE ti=?1",[ti])?;
   598	            let work=crate::rows::adoption::Work::table(table);
   599	            let scratch=|row:&Value|crate::rows::adoption::contribution_bytes(row,table);
   600	            super::activation_walk::walk_metered(tx,tx.num(name)?,work,if work.index_entries!=0 {Some(&scratch)}else{None},|id,mut row,_|{
   601	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
   602	                for key in index_keys(definition,&row,id){tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO i(ti,k,id) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",params![ti,key,id])?;}Ok(())
   603	            })?;
   604	        }
   605	    }
   606	    if tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_cap_counts')",[],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))? {
   607	        tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _cap_counts",[])?;
   608	        for name in &names {let table=&source.tables[name];super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|_,row|tx.update_cap_counts(name,table,None,Some(&row)).map(|_|()))?;}
   609	    }
   610	    validate_action_bounds(tx,&source,now)?;
   611	    tx.clear_activation_scan();
   612	    for name in &names {
   613	        let t=tx.num(name)?;
   614	        super::activation::walk(tx,t,|id,_|{
   615	            let Some(mut row)=tx.get(name,id)? else{return Ok(())};
   616	            if !source_expired(tx,t,id,&source.tables[name],&row,now)? {return Ok(())}
   617	            materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&source.tables[name]);
   618	            let mut runner=Runner::new(tx,&source,Context::system().at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort),tx.next_seq()?);
   619	            match runner.retire_expired_row(name,&row) {
   620	                Ok(true)=>{tx.set_meta("activation_population","true")?;},
   621	                Ok(false)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id} is blocked by retained children; resolve them in a bridge before adoption"))),
   622	                Err(e)=>return Err(refusal(format!("source expiry {name}/{id}: {e}"))),
   623	            }Ok(())
   624	        })?;
   625	    }
   626	    for name in &names {
   627	        let cleared:Vec<_>=source.tables[name].on_delete.iter().filter(|(_,a)|**a==crate::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull).map(|(c,_)|c.clone()).collect();
   628	        if cleared.is_empty(){continue;}
   629	        super::activation::walk(tx,tx.num(name)?,|id,row|{
   630	            for c in &cleared {if row.field(c).is_ok_and(|v|*v==Value::Null){
   631	                let t=tx.num(name)?;
   632	                tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4",params![canonical_json(&Value::Null),t,id,c])?;
   633	                let bytes:Option<i64>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT octet_length(data) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   634	                let mut admission=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);admission.heap((bytes.unwrap_or(0).max(0) as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(row.retained_heap_bytes().saturating_mul(64)).saturating_add(8192))?;
   635	                let archived:Option<String>=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
   636	                if let Some(archived)=archived {let mut image:Value=decode(&archived)?;if let Value::Row(fields)|Value::Map(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert(c.clone(),Value::Null);}tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3",params![canonical_json(&image),t,id])?;}
   637	                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",params![t,id,c])?;
   638	            }}Ok(())
   639	        })?;
   640	    }

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev" && body.devDoors !== false) {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}

  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // Native persistence is the default. --no-memory remains a consumed explicit alias.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory") });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");

/** A bounded directly spawned build/tool command, with the same file logs. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export async function command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  const run = logged(binary, args, { ...options, env: { ...process.env, ...options.env } });
  try {
    await bounded(run.closed, deadline(options.timeoutMs, 120_000), "proof cli close");
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    return { code: run.child.exitCode, signal: run.child.signalCode, stdout: run.stdout(), stderr: logText(run.logs.stderr), logs: run.logs };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
  finally { run.child.stdout?.destroy(); } // Secret capture must not pin the driver after a deadline.
}
async function successful(args: string[], options: ProofOptions) {
  const result = await cli(args, options);
  if (result.code !== 0) {
    const stdout = result.stdout.trimEnd();
    const tail = stdout.length > 4000 ? `…${stdout.slice(-4000)}` : stdout;
    throw Error(`proof CLI failed (${result.code}): ${result.stderr}; stdout tail: ${tail || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stderr}`);
  }
  return result;
}
/** @throws missing explicit saved source, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (typeof schema !== "string" || !schema) throw Error(`proof deploy requires an explicit saved schema file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(schema) ?? String(schema)}: did npm run day2:proof pass versions/v1?`);
  return successful(["deploy", dir, "--schema", schema, ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
/** @throws invalid explicit release target, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function rollback(dir: string, to: number | "latest", options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (to !== "latest" && (!Number.isSafeInteger(to) || to < 1)) throw Error(`proof rollback requires an explicit immutable release target; got ${JSON.stringify(to) ?? String(to)}`);
  return successful(["rollback", dir, "--to", String(to), ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — saved-source proof results expose authored cases separately from query sweeps.
export interface TestSourceOptions extends ProofOptions { fixtureOnly?: boolean }
export interface TestSourceResult extends CliResult { casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number }
/** @throws missing source, CLI failure, invalid business-case summary or zero cases without fixtureOnly. */
export function testSource(source: string, options: TestSourceOptions = {}): Promise<TestSourceResult> {
  if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) throw Error(`proof testSource requires an explicit saved source file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(source) ?? String(source)}: pass versions/v1`);
  const { fixtureOnly = false, ...proofOptions } = options;
  return successful(["test", "--source", source, ...(fixtureOnly ? ["--fixture-only"] : []), ...(proofOptions.args ?? [])], proofOptions).then(result => {
    const summaries = [...result.stdout.matchAll(/^SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES (\{.*\})$/gm)];
    if (summaries.length !== 1) throw Error(`proof testSource received no unique business-case summary; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    let cases: { run: number; skipped: number };
    try { cases = JSON.parse(summaries[0]![1]!) as { run: number; skipped: number }; }
    catch { throw Error(`proof testSource received a malformed business-case summary: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`); }
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cases.run) || cases.run < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(cases.skipped) || cases.skipped < 0) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource received invalid business-case counts: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    }
    if (cases.run === 0 && !fixtureOnly) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource ran no business cases (${cases.skipped} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl for this saved version, or pass { fixtureOnly: true } when fixture loading alone is the intended proof; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}`);
    }
    return { ...result, casesRun: cases.run, casesSkipped: cases.skipped };
  });
}

/** @throws None; returns a boolean for recognized sandbox launch errors. */
export function sandboxUnavailable(error: unknown): boolean {
  return /No usable sandbox|Running as root without --no-sandbox|Failed to move to new namespace|SUID sandbox|sandbox.*Operation not permitted|Failed to unshare.*Operation not permitted/i.test(String(error));
}
/** @throws E_PROOF_BROWSER for missing playwright-core; invalid deadline, browser launch failure or callback error; bounded context/browser cleanup failure. */
export async function withBrowser<T>(options: LaunchOptions & Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> & { chromium?: BrowserType }, proof: (context: BrowserContext) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const { chromium: suppliedChromium, executablePath, cwd: _cwd, logsDir: _logs, ...other } = options;
  const requireCaller = createRequire(resolve(options.cwd ?? process.cwd(), "package.json"));
  let caller = suppliedChromium;
  if (!caller) {
    for (const peer of ["playwright-core", "@playwright/test", "playwright"]) {
      let path: string;
      try { path = requireCaller.resolve(peer); }
      catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "MODULE_NOT_FOUND") continue; throw error; }
      const peerModule = await import(path);
      caller = (peerModule.chromium ?? peerModule.default?.chromium) as BrowserType;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!caller) {
    try { caller = (await import("playwright-core")).chromium; }
    catch (cause) { throw new Error("E_PROOF_BROWSER: withBrowser requires playwright-core; run npm install --save-dev playwright-core before deploying or starting proof hosts", { cause }); }
  }
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const err = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.browser.stderr.log`), "wx", 0o600);
  const diagnostic = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.witness.private.jsonl`), "wx", 0o600);

  const launch = { ...other, ...(executablePath === undefined ? {} : { executablePath }), timeout: deadline(options.timeout, 30_000), logger: {
    isEnabled: () => true,
    log: (name: string, severity: string, message: string | Error) => { writeSync(err, `${name} ${severity}: ${String(message)}\n`); },
  } };
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (process.getuid?.() === 0 && !args.includes("--no-sandbox")) args.push("--no-sandbox");
  // Playwright owns its browser child over a pipe, including parent-exit cleanup.
  // No WebSocket server or TCP debugging port is opened.
  let browser;
  try {
    try { browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args, chromiumSandbox: !args.includes("--no-sandbox") }); }
    catch (error) {
      if (args.includes("--no-sandbox") || !sandboxUnavailable(error)) throw error;
      browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args: [...args, "--no-sandbox"], chromiumSandbox: false });
    }
    let context: BrowserContext | undefined;
    try {
      context = await browser.newContext();
      await context.exposeBinding("__snapback4ProofDiagnostic", (_source, value: unknown) => {
        writeSync(diagnostic, `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`);
      });
      // Init scripts run on each document, including HTTP redirect destinations,
      // before any application script samples its URL.
      await context.addInitScript(() => {
        if (window.top !== window || !/^https?:$/.test(location.protocol)) return;
        const url = new URL(location.href);
        url.searchParams.set("witness", "web");
        history.replaceState(history.state, "", url.href);
      });
      context.setDefaultTimeout(30_000); context.setDefaultNavigationTimeout(30_000);
      return await proof(context);
    } finally {
      try { if (context) await bounded(context.close(), 5000, "browser context close"); }
      finally { await bounded(browser.close(), 5000, "browser close"); }
    }
  } finally { closeSync(err); closeSync(diagnostic); }
}
const substringLocators = new WeakMap<Locator, { locator: Locator; scoped: boolean }>();
/** Strings match exactly by default; use exact:false or a RegExp for a row with more text. Excludes editable values. */
/** @throws None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. */
export function renderedText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp, options: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean } = {}): Locator {
  const root = options.within ?? page, rendered = page.locator(":not(input):not(textarea)");
  const locator = root.getByText(text, { exact: options.exact ?? true }).and(rendered);
  if (typeof text === "string" && options.exact !== false) substringLocators.set(locator, { locator: root.getByText(text, { exact: false }).and(rendered), scoped: options.within !== undefined });
  return locator;
}

                crate::store::release_history::event(tx,&options.operation,Some(options.release),options.expected_previous,serde_json::json!({"dormant_items":dormant_items,"restored_items":restored_items,"native_digest":options.native_digest,"narrowed_retained":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"narrowed_restored":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)}),&items)?;
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key IN ('activation_restore','activation_population','activation_narrowed_retained','activation_narrowed_restored') OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%'",[])?;
            }
            crate::store::opening::Metadata::read(tx)
        });
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Some((retained,restored))=population.get().filter(|(a,b)|*a>0 || *b>0) {
                store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0,format!("{retained} newly dormant {}; {restored} narrowed {} restored", if retained == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }, if restored == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
            }
        }
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Ok(items)=store.dormant_inventory(None,usize::MAX) {
                if release.is_some() { store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0, crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items)); }
            }
        }
        outcome
    }
}

- **[P2] Unrelated pruned history blocks valid native re-entry.** [backfills.rs:219](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b17-mig/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/backfills.rs:219) treats **any** pruned receipt on the row as conflicting, without checking output overlap.

  Reproduction: insert a native row while declaration G owns `b`; later introduce F transforming only `a`; prune F’s evidence; remove G while retaining `b`; then re-enter G. Despite G’s unchanged fingerprint and complete valid `b`, activation refuses because F’s tombstone exists.

  This contradicts §3’s native-image preservation and overlapping-history rules. Check pruned definitions for overlapping outputs, preserving refusal for actual conflicts, and add this regression.
